def conv_corr(w, p, M_fft):
    W = np.fft.rfft(w, M_fft) # w length L, real
    P = np.fft.rfft(p, M_fft)
    C = np.fft.irfft(W * np.conj(P), M_fft)  # is this C_i=∑ w[j+i] p[j]? Need verify sign with rfft.

Fast score/c:
```
def linear_convolution_square(p, M):
    F = np.fft.rfft(p, M)
    c = np.fft.irfft(F*F, M)[:2*n-1]
    # remove tiny neg
    c[c<0]=0
    return c
```
Since p sum1, sum c ~1. For gradient, c may have tiny errors; max maybe.

Correlation:
```
def corr_w_p(w, p, M):
    Fw=np.fft.rfft(w, M)
    Fp=np.fft.rfft(p, M)
    c=np.fft.irfft(Fw * np.conj(Fp), M)
    return c[:len(p)]
```

```
def run_optimizer(p0, budget, seed, rng, stop_score=None):
    n=len(p0)
    p=np.abs(p0).astype(float)
    psum=p.sum()
    if psum <=0: p=np.ones(n)/n
    p/=psum
    M=next_pow2(2*n-1)
    best=p.copy()
    best_score=score_fft(p)
    beta=0.35
    reject_count=0
    phase=0
    t_end=time+ budget
    it=0
    while time < t_end:
        it+=1
        current = best (or keep current? We'll always use best for line search)
        c=conv_sq(p? maybe best) # p current
        sc=2n*maxc
        # if sc < best: update best
        # select r:
        if it < 20: r=4
        elif it < 60: r=8
        elif it < 150: r=16
        elif it < 300: r=32
        else: r random from [16,32,64,128]? or 32.
        # compute D
        D = gradient_power(p,c,r,M)
        Dn = D / (np.std(D)+eps)
        # maybe clip Dn to [-5,5]
        # Add random if reject_count large
        if reject_count>5:
             z=rng.normal(0, 0.05, n)
             Dn += z / std(z)
        # Try betas: base beta, maybe beta*1.5, beta*2? We can evaluate best beta among a small set.
        candidates=[]
        for gamma in [1, 1.6, 0.6]:
            lf = -gamma*beta*Dn
            lf = np.clip(lf, -4, 4)
            p_try = p * np.exp(lf)
            p_try = np.maximum(p_try, 1e-15)
            s=p_try.sum()
            if s <=0: ...
            p_try /= s
            sc_try=score_fft(p_try)
            if sc_try < best_score - 1e-12: accept...
        if not accept:
             reject_count +=1; beta *=0.85
             # if beta<0.02, add perturbation or reset
        else:
             best=p_accepted; best_score=...
             beta = min(1.5, beta*1.05)
             p=best # for next
             reject_count=0
             if best_score < stop_score: break
        # occasional random perturbation of best and accept if better
        if it % 50==0:
             p_rand = perturb(best, rng)
             sc_rand=score_fft(p_rand)
             if sc_rand<best_score: update
    return best,best_score
```

Random perturbation:
```
def random_perturb(p, rng, strength):
    n=len(p)
    t=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    logp=np.log(p)
    # low-freq consines
    for k in range(3):
       coeff=rng.normal(0,0.1)
       logp += coeff*np.cos(2π(k+1)t + rng.normal(0,1))
    # block multiplies
    for _ in range(5): width...
    return exp(logp - max) normalized
```

```

    starts=[]
    for profile in profiles:
       p=generate
       sc=score_fft(p)
       starts.append((sc,p))
    sort by score

```
def _optimize_from_start(p0, deadline, seed):
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    p=_clean_normalize(p0)
    n=len(p)
    M=_next_pow2(2*n-1)
    best=p.copy()
    best_score=_score_fft(best)
    beta=0.25
    reject_count=0
    it=0
    last_improve=time.monotonic()
    # optional r cycles
    r_cycle=[4,8,16,32,16,32,64,32,64,128]
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       it+=1
       if it > 50 and time.monotonic()-last_improve > 60: # stuck
           # perturb best
           p2=_random_perturb(best, rng, 0.1)
           sc2=_score_fft(p2)
           if sc2 < best_score:
               best=p2; best_score=sc2; last_improve=...
           else:
               # accept perturbation anyway with small prob? maybe if no improvement for long
               if reject_count > 50 or rng.random()<0.05:
                   best=p2; # do not update best_score? but need current for optimization. We can set p=p2 and keep best for return. But line search from worse may not. Maybe skip.
       r = r_cycle[min(it//10, len-1)] maybe
       p=best.copy()? We need current for D. Use p=best (not modify before trials).
       c=_fft_conv_square(p,M,n)
       maxc=np.max(c)
       if maxc <=0: break
       cur_score=2*n*maxc
       if cur_score < best_score:
           best=p; best_score=cur_score; last_improve=...
       Dn=_gradient_power_direction(p,c,M,r)
       if Dn is None: break
       # try betas
       trials=[0.5*beta, beta, 1.5*beta]
       best_trial=None; best_trial_score=cur_score
       for b in trials:
           if b <1e-5: continue
           pf=_trial_update(p,Dn,b)
           sf=_score_fft(pf)
           if sf < best_trial_score:
               best_trial=pf; best_trial_score=sf
       if best_trial is not None:
           best=best_trial; best_score=best_trial_score; p=best.copy(); last_improve=...
           beta=min(2.0, beta*1.1)
           reject_count=0
       else:
           reject_count+=1
           beta=max(0.02, beta*0.85)
           # if very stuck, use random direction on Dn? or add noise and retry
           if reject_count > 25:
               # do a random perturbation and if better accept, else larger noise added to Dn
               noise = rng.normal(0,0.1,n)
               Dn = Dn + noise/(np.std(noise)+1e-12)
               Dn -= Dn.mean()
               scale=np.sqrt(np.mean(Dn*Dn)); if scale>1e-12: Dn/=scale
               reject_count=0
               beta=0.5
    return best, best_score
```

```
import numpy as np
import time

def _clean_seq_arr(x):
    arr=np.asarray(x,dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    arr=np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr=np.clip(arr,0.0,1000.0)
    return arr
```

`_normalize_nonnegative(p, min_p=1e-12):`
```
p=np.asarray(p,dtype=float)
p=np.where(np.isfinite(p),p,0.0)
p=np.clip(p,0.0,1000.0)
if p.size==0: p=np.ones(1)
s=p.sum()
if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-15:
   p=np.ones(p.size)/p.size
else:
   p = p/s
p = np.maximum(p, min_p)
s=p.sum()
if s>0: p/=s
return p
```
If set min_p 1e-12 and n=12000, sum increases by 1e-8 negligible. But if p has many zeros, p floor; okay.

`_next_pow2(m)`.

`_fft_conv_square(p, M, n)`:
```
F=np.fft.rfft(p, M)
c=np.fft.irfft(F*F, M)[:2*n-1]
c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
return c
```
Potential for M too small if n=0? n≥1. For n=1, L=1, M=1? rfft with M=1 okay. But we don't use n=1? Could. `_next_pow2(1)=1`.

`_fft_corr(w, p, M)`:
```
Fw=np.fft.rfft(w, M)
Fp=np.fft.rfft(p, M)
c=np.fft.irfft(Fw*np.conj(Fp), M)
return c[:len(p)]
```
Could have tiny negative; later max.

`_score_fft(p, M=None)`:
```
p=np.asarray(p)
s=p.sum()
if s<=0: return np.inf
n=len(p)
if M is None: M=_next_pow2(2*n-1)
c=_fft_conv_square(p,M,n)
return float(2.0*n*np.max(c)/(s*s))
```
In optimizer p sum1; we can use `_score_normalized(c,n)=2n maxc`. For trial, p normalized sum1 but due floor sum maybe 1. Use `_score_fft` robust.

But `_score_fft` recomputes c; in optimizer current c already. For trial, okay.

`_gradient_power_direction(p,c,M,r)`:
```
n=len(p)
mc=float(np.max(c))
if mc <=0 or not finite: return None
u=c/mc
L=2*n-1
# r float
if r <= 1.0: # use direct subgradient? return D maybe random? 
    # use r=1 not useful; set r=2 fallback
    r=2.0
# w
if r == 1.0:
    w=np.ones(L)
    Hs=float(np.sum(u))
else:
    # To avoid underflow/overflow, use power
    up=r-1.0
    if up == 1:
       w=u.copy()
    else:
       w=np.power(u, up)
    Hr=np.power(u, r)
    Hs=float(np.sum(Hr))
Ts=_fft_corr(w,p,M)
Ts=np.maximum(Ts,0.0)
D = p*(Ts - mc*Hs)
# center and normalize
D = D - np.mean(D)
var=float(np.mean(D*D))
if not finite or var<1e-24:
    # maybe fallback random
    return None
D /= np.sqrt(var)
# clip
D = np.clip(D, -8.0, 8.0)
# re-center maybe
D = D - np.mean(D)
if np.std(D) not... # if all zero return None
return D
```
Should we use `mc*Hs` or normalized? Yes.
If r high, `Hr` underflow; Hs maybe 1. If all u=0 except one, w one, Ts etc. Fine.
If `Ts - mcHs` all ~0, var small; return None. Then optimizer can random.

`_trial_update(p,Dn,beta,min_p=1e-12)`:
```
lf = -beta * Dn
lf = np.clip(lf, -8.0, 8.0)
pf = p * np.exp(lf)
pf = np.clip(pf, min_p, None)
s=pf.sum()
if s <=0: return p.copy()
pf /= s
return pf
```
If Dn has mean0 but clip distorts; sum not 1 before normalize. Good.

`_random_multiplicative_perturb(p, rng, strength=0.2)`:
```
n=len(p); t=np.linspace(0,1,n)
lp=np.log(np.maximum(p,1e-300))
for f in (1,2,3,5,8):
   amp = rng.normal(0, strength / 3.0)
   phase = rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
   lp += amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*f*t + phase)
# block
num_blocks = max(3, n//400)
for _ in range(num_blocks):
   width = int(rng.integers(max(2,n//1000), max(3,n//20)))
   start=int(rng.integers(0,max(1,n-width)))
   amp=rng.normal(0, strength/2)

p2=np.exp(lp - np.max(lp))

return a

Now `_optimize_from_start` details no lambdas:
```
def _optimize_from_start(p0, deadline, seed):
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    p=_normalize_nonnegative(p0,1e-12)
    best=p.copy()
    best_score=_score_fft(best)
    current=p.copy()
    current_score=best_score
    M=_next_pow2(2*len(p)-1)
    beta=0.25
    rejects=0
    no_improve_time=time.monotonic()
    last_best_print=0
    step_count=0
    r_schedule=[8,8,16,16,16,32,32,32,64,64,128,64,128,128]
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       step_count +=1
       step_idx = step_count % len(r_schedule)
       r = float(r_schedule[step_idx])
       # optional random perturbation if long no improvement
       if time.monotonic() - no_improve_time > 90:
           p_rand=_random_multiplicative_perturb(best, rng, 0.15)
           sc_rand=_score_fft(p_rand, M)
           if sc_rand < best_score:
               best=p_rand.copy(); best_score=sc_rand; no_improve_time=time.monotonic()
           else:
               # don't update best, maybe add noise later
               pass
       c=_fft_conv_square(current, M, len(current))
       maxc=float(np.max(c))
       cur_score=2.0*len(current)*maxc
       if cur_score < current_score - 1e-13:
           current_score = cur_score
       if cur_score < best_score - 1e-13:
           best=current.copy(); best_score=cur_score; no_improve_time=time.monotonic()
       Dn=_gradient_power_direction(current, c, M, r)
       if Dn is None:
           # fallback random perturb current and maybe evaluate
           p_rand=_random_multiplicative_perturb(current, rng, 0.2)
           sc_rand=_score_fft(p_rand,M)
           if sc_rand < best_score:
               best=p_rand.copy(); best_score=sc_rand; no_improve_time=time.monotonic()
           rejects += 1
           beta=0.25
           continue
       # occasional add noise to Dn when many rejects
       if rejects > 8:
           noise_scale = min(1.0, 0.05 * rejects)
           noise = rng.normal(0.0, noise_scale, len(current))
           stdn = float(np.std(noise))
           if stdn > 1e-8:
               Dn = Dn + noise/stdn
               Dn = Dn - np.mean(Dn)
               sD = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(Dn*Dn)))
               if sD > 1e-12:
                   Dn /= sD
                   Dn = np.clip(Dn,-8,8)
       # try betas
       betas = [0.5*beta, beta, 1.5*beta]
       # ensure positive
       best_trial=None; best_trial_score=cur_score
       for b in betas:
           if b <= 1e-6: continue
           pf=_trial_update(current, Dn, b)
           sf=_score_fft(pf, M)
           if sf < best_trial_score - 1e-13:
               best_trial=pf; best_trial_score=sf
       if best_trial is not None:
           current=best_trial
           current_score=best_trial_score
           rejects=0
           beta = min(1.8, beta*1.08)
           if current_score < best_score:
               best=current.copy(); best_score=current_score; no_improve_time=time.monotonic()
       else:
           rejects +=1
           beta = max(0.03, beta*0.85)
           # If many rejects, maybe reset beta
           if rejects == 20:
               beta = 0.5
           elif rejects == 40:
               beta = 0.2
       # If current has drifted? It is always best if improvements; no drift.
    return best, best_score
```
Since current=best whenever improvement accepted? We set current to trial if improves current, but if trial is not best but better than current? Wait current initially best. We only accept if `sf < best_trial_score` initialized to `cur_score` (current_score). Since current is often best, this is improvement over current. If current is best, accepted only if best improves. So current always best. Good. We don't allow worsening moves. The random perturbation if improves best sets best but not current? We should set current=best after random improvement. Add `current=best.copy(); current_score=best_score;`. Good. In Dn None fallback, if improves, set current. Do.

If many rejects from current=best, it keeps trying noise direction. Good.

The loop recomputes c for current=best every iteration even if no improvement; Dn same unless noise/r. Rejects change Dn with noise. Fine.

The no_improve_time initially start; if no improvement, after 90s random perturb. But rejects likely many before.

Potential issue: `best_trial_score=cur_score` uses FFT score from current c (normalized). `_score_fft(pf)` computes sum p maybe 1; okay. If trial score equal within 1e-13, reject. Could stagnate. Use no threshold? `sf < best_trial_score` exact. Use -1e-15? We'll use `< best_trial_score` without epsilon to accept tiny; but floating. Fine.

def _profile_sequence(n, alpha, beta):
    c=np.empty(n)

       t=np.linspace(0,1,n-1) # for k=1..

    # recurrence
    for k in range(1,n):

          prev=a[1:k]  # length k-1
          # cross = dot(prev, prev[::-1])? Need sum_{i=1}^{k-1} a_i a_{k-i}

```python
"""
Multiplicative log-gradient search for non-negative sequences minimizing
    2*n*max(conv(a,a))/sum(a)^2.

The implementation keeps the sequence normalized to sum 1 and minimizes a
smooth surrogate  (1/r) log sum_k c_k^r - 2 log sum(a), where c = a*a.
Large r emphasizes the dominant convolution coefficients.  Gradient components
are computed with FFT correlation, and candidate steps are accepted by exact
FFT score.  Multiple asymmetric starts are used, including central-binomial
prefixes, shifted/tail-lifted profiles, power laws, recurrence-generated
profile starts for moderate n, and optionally a re-ordered external sequence.
"""

import numpy as np
import time


def _clean_seq_arr(x):
    """Convert a candidate sequence to a finite non-negative float array."""
    arr = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    return arr


def _normalize_nonnegative(p, min_p=1e-12):
    """Return a positive finite sequence normalized to sum 1."""
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if p.size == 0:
        p = np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if (not np.isfinite(s)) or s <= 1e-15:
        p = np.ones(p.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(p.size)
    else:
        p = p / s
    p = np.maximum(p, min_p)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if s > 0.0 and np.isfinite(s):
        p = p / s
    return p


def _next_pow2(m):
    """Smallest power of two >= m."""
    m = int(m)
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def _fft_conv_square(p, M, n):
    """Linear convolution p*p by zero-padded FFT, length 2n-1."""
    F = np.fft.rfft(p, M)
    c = np.fft.irfft(F * F, M)[: (2 * n - 1)]
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    return c


def _fft_corr(w, p, M):
    """
    Correlation T_i = sum_j p_j w_{i+j} for i=0..len(p)-1.
    Uses zero-padding to M; valid for i < len(p) when M >= len(w).
    """
    Fw = np.fft.rfft(w, M)
    Fp = np.fft.rfft(p, M)
    corr = np.fft.irfft(Fw * np.conj(Fp), M)
    return corr[: len(p)]


def _score_fft(p, M=None):
    """Objective 2*n*max(c)/sum(p)^2 using FFT."""
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    n = p.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf
    s = float(p.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.inf
    if M is None:
        M = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    c = _fft_conv_square(p, M, n)
    mc = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(mc):
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * n * mc / (s * s))


def _gradient_power_direction(p, c, M, r):
    """
    Return normalized log-space gradient direction for
    F_r = (1/r) log(sum c^r) - 2 log(sum p).
    A positive direction should be subtracted from log(p).
    """
    n = p.size
    mc = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(mc) or mc <= 0.0:
        return None

    u = c / mc
    L = 2 * n - 1

    if r < 2.0:
        r = 2.0

    if r == 2.0:
        w = u.copy()
    else:
        w = np.power(u, r - 1.0)

    Hr = np.power(u, r)
    Hs = float(np.sum(Hr))
    if not np.isfinite(Hs) or Hs <= 0.0:
        return None

    Ts = _fft_corr(w, p, M)
    Ts = np.maximum(Ts, 0.0)

    D = p * (Ts - mc * Hs)
    D = D - float(np.mean(D))
    var = float(np.mean(D * D))
    if not np.isfinite(var) or var <= 1e-24:
        return None

    D = D / np.sqrt(var)
    D = np.clip(D, -8.0, 8.0)
    D = D - float(np.mean(D))
    sd = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(D * D)))
    if not np.isfinite(sd) or sd <= 1e-12:
        return None
    D = D / sd
    return D


def _trial_update(p, Dn, beta):
    """Multiplicative log-step and renormalization."""
    lf = -float(beta) * Dn
    lf = np.clip(lf, -8.0, 8.0)
    pf = p * np.exp(lf)
    pf = np.clip(pf, 1e-12, None)
    s = float(pf.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return p.copy()
    pf = pf / s
    return pf


def _theta_update(p, theta):
    """Multiply by a smooth linear-in-index exponential lift and renormalize."""
    n = p.size
    if n == 1:
        return p.copy()
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    q = p * np.exp(float(theta) * (t - 0.5))
    return _normalize_nonnegative(q, 1e-12)


def _random_multiplicative_perturb(p, rng, strength=0.15):
    """Random smooth/block multiplicative perturbation."""
    n = p.size
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    lp = np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-300))

    for f in (1, 2, 3, 5, 8):
        amp = rng.normal(0.0, strength / 3.0)
        phase = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)
        lp = lp + amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * f * t + phase)

    num_blocks = max(3, n // 400)
    for _ in range(num_blocks):
        lo = max(2, n // 2000)
        hi = max(lo + 1, n // 50)
        width = int(rng.integers(lo, hi))
        start = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - width + 1)))
        amp = rng.normal(0.0, strength / 2.0)
        lp[start:start + width] += amp

    q = np.exp(lp - np.max(lp))
    return _normalize_nonnegative(q, 1e-12)


def _prefix_sequence(n):
    """
    Coefficients of (1-x)^(-1/2): central binomial / 4^k.
    This infinite sequence has flat convolution 1/(1-x); truncation gives
    an excellent asymmetric start with score ~ pi/2.
    """
    if n <= 0:
        return np.ones(0)
    a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if n > 1:
        k = np.arange(1, n, dtype=np.float64)
        a[1:] = np.cumprod((2.0 * k - 1.0) / (2.0 * k))
    return a


def _power_law_sequence(n, q):
    if n <= 0:
        return np.ones(0)
    x = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    return np.power(x, -float(q))


def _profile_sequence(n, alpha, beta):
    """
    Generate an asymmetric start by prescribing the first n convolution
    coefficients c_k (k=0..n-1) and taking their formal square root.
    Only used for moderate n because of O(n^2) recurrence.
    """
    if n <= 1:
        return np.ones(max(1, n))

    c = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    alpha = float(np.clip(alpha, 0.05, 1.0))
    c[0] = alpha
    if n > 1:
        x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n - 1)
        c[1:] = 1.0 - (1.0 - alpha) * np.exp(-float(beta) * x)

    a0 = float(np.sqrt(alpha))
    a = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a[0] = a0

    for k in range(1, n):
        if k == 1:
            cross = 0.0
        else:
            prev = a[1:k]
            cross = float(np.dot(prev, prev[::-1]))
        s = float(c[k] - cross)
        if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
            s = 1e-14
        a[k] = s / (2.0 * a0)

    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return _normalize_nonnegative(_prefix_sequence(n), 1e-12)
    return _normalize_nonnegative(a / s, 1e-12)


def _get_height_sequence():
    """Safely fetch the external height_sequence_1 global, if present."""
    try:
        raw = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
        if raw is None:
            return None
        arr = _clean_seq_arr(raw)
        if arr.size == 0:
            return None
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(arr)):
            return None
        return arr
    except Exception:
        return None


def _external_start(n, sorted_descending=False):
    """Use external sequence only if its length matches."""
    raw = _get_height_sequence()
    if raw is None or raw.size != n:
        return None
    if sorted_descending:
        p = np.sort(raw)[::-1]
    else:
        p = raw.copy()
    return _normalize_nonnegative(p, 1e-12)


def _generate_starts(n, seed):
    """Generate and score a small pool of asymmetric starts for length n."""
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    M = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    candidates = []

    pref = _prefix_sequence(n)
    snap = _normalize_nonnegative(pref, 1e-12)
    candidates.append(snap)

    for theta in (0.3, 0.6, 1.0, 1.6, -0.4, -0.8):
        candidates.append(_theta_update(snap, theta))

    for q in (0.45, 0.50, 0.55):
        candidates.append(_normalize_nonnegative(_power_law_sequence(n, q), 1e-12))

    if n <= 5000:
        for alpha in (0.82, 0.90):
            for beta in (1.0, 2.5, 5.0):
                candidates.append(_profile_sequence(n, alpha, beta))

    ext0 = _external_start(n, False)
    if ext0 is not None:
        candidates.append(ext0)
    ext1 = _external_start(n, True)
    if ext1 is not None:
        candidates.append(ext1)

    scored = []
    for p in candidates:
        if p is None or p.size != n:
            continue
        p = _normalize_nonnegative(p, 1e-12)
        sc = _score_fft(p, M)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            scored.append((sc, p))

    if not scored:
        scored.append((_score_fft(snap, M), snap))

    scores = np.array([x[0] for x in scored], dtype=np.float64)
    order = np.argsort(scores)
    return [scored[i] for i in order]


def _optimize_from_start(p0, deadline, seed):
    """Local ruthlessly-descent optimizer with score-based line search."""
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    p = _normalize_nonnegative(p0, 1e-12)
    n = p.size
    M = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)

    best = p.copy()
    best_score = _score_fft(best, M)
    current = p.copy()
    current_score = best_score

    beta = 0.25
    rejects = 0
    step_count = 0
    last_improve_time = time.monotonic()
    last_print_score = best_score

    r_schedule = [8.0, 8.0, 16.0, 16.0, 16.0,
                  32.0, 32.0, 32.0, 64.0, 64.0,
                  128.0, 64.0, 128.0, 128.0]

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        step_count += 1
        r = r_schedule[step_count % len(r_schedule)]

        # Periodic random restart from the best sequence.
        if time.monotonic() - last_improve_time > 90.0:
            prand = _random_multiplicative_perturb(best, rng, 0.18)
            srand = _score_fft(prand, M)
            if srand < best_score:
                best = prand.copy()
                best_score = srand
                current = best.copy()
                current_score = best_score
                last_improve_time = time.monotonic()
                rejects = 0
                beta = 0.25

        c = _fft_conv_square(current, M, n)
        maxc = float(np.max(c))
        cur_score = float(2 * n * maxc)
        if cur_score < current_score:
            current_score = cur_score

        if cur_score < best_score:
            best = current.copy()
            best_score = cur_score
            last_improve_time = time.monotonic()

        if best_score < last_print_score - 0.0015 or step_count % 200 == 0:
            print(f"  [opt n={n} step={step_count}] fft_score={best_score:.8f} beta={beta:.3f}")
            last_print_score = best_score

        Dn = _gradient_power_direction(current, c, M, r)

        if Dn is None:
            prand = _random_multiplicative_perturb(current, rng, 0.25)
            srand = _score_fft(prand, M)
            if srand < best_score:
                best = prand.copy()
                best_score = srand
                current = best.copy()
                current_score = best_score
                last_improve_time = time.monotonic()
            rejects += 1
            beta = 0.25
            continue

        # If gradient line search stalls, inject coordinate/log-space noise.
        if rejects > 8:
            noise_scale = min(1.0, 0.05 * float(rejects))
            noise = rng.normal(0.0, noise_scale, n)
            ns = float(np.std(noise))
            if ns > 1e-8:
                Dn = Dn + noise / ns
                Dn = Dn - float(np.mean(Dn))
                ds = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(Dn * Dn)))
                if ds > 1e-12:
                    Dn = Dn / ds
                    Dn = np.clip(Dn, -8.0, 8.0)

        # Deterministic linear lift/drop tries: often very effective for this
        # asymmetric convolution objective.
        if step_count % 40 == 0:
            for theta in (0.05, -0.05, 0.12, -0.12):
                pth = _theta_update(current, theta)
                sth = _score_fft(pth, M)
                if sth < cur_score:
                    current = pth
                    current_score = sth
                    rejects = 0
                    if sth < best_score:
                        best = current.copy()
                        best_score = sth
                        last_improve_time = time.monotonic()
                    break

        trial_scores = []
        trial_seqs = []
        for b in (0.5 * beta, beta, 1.5 * beta):
            if b <= 1e-6:
                continue
            pf = _trial_update(current, Dn, b)
            sf = _score_fft(pf, M)
            trial_scores.append((sf, pf))
            if not trial_seqs:
                pass

        if trial_seqs:
            pass

        # Choose best trial by score; compare to current.
        best_trial = None
        best_trial_score = cur_score
        for item in trial_scores:
            if item[0] < best_trial_score:
                best_trial_score = item[0]
                best_trial = item[1]

        if best_trial is not None:
            current = best_trial
            current_score = best_trial_score
            rejects = 0
            beta = min(1.8, beta * 1.08)
            if current_score < best_score:
                best = current.copy()
                best_score = current_score
                last_improve_time = time.monotonic()
        else:
            rejects += 1
            beta = max(0.03, beta * 0.85)
            if rejects == 20:
                beta = 0.5
            elif rejects == 40:
                beta = 0.2

    return best, best_score


def _search_length(n, stage_deadline, seed):
    """Search a fixed length using several starts."""
    n = int(n)
    if n < 64:
        return None, np.inf

    starts = _generate_starts(n, seed)
    if not starts:
        return None, np.inf

    top = starts[:3]
    best_seq, best_score = top[0][1], top[0][0]

    now = time.monotonic()
    total = max(1.0, float(stage_deadline - now))
    if total < 10.0:
        return best_seq, best_score

    if len(top) == 1:
        fractions = [1.0]
    elif len(top) == 2:
        fractions = [0.70, 0.30]
    else:
        fractions = [0.60, 0.28, 0.12]

    idx = 0
    while idx < len(top) and time.monotonic() < stage_deadline:
        rem = float(stage_deadline - time.monotonic())
        if rem < 15.0:
            break
        chunk = max(15.0, rem * fractions[idx])
        inner_deadline = min(float(stage_deadline), time.monotonic() + chunk)

        seq, sc = _optimize_from_start(top[idx][1], inner_deadline, seed + 1000 * idx)
        if seq is not None and np.isfinite(sc):
            if sc < best_score:
                best_seq, best_score = seq, sc
        idx += 1

    return best_seq, best_score


def _stage_lengths(budget, external_n):
    """Choose length stages and rough time fractions."""
    budget = float(budget)

    if budget < 700.0:
        return [(4096, 1.0)]

    if budget < 1500.0:
        if external_n is not None and 3000 <= int(external_n) <= 9000:
            return [(int(external_n), 0.45), (8192, 0.55)]
        return [(4096, 0.40), (8192, 0.60)]

    if external_n is not None and 4000 <= int(external_n) <= 10000:
        return [(2048, 0.05), (int(external_n), 0.42), (12000, 0.53)]

    return [(2048, 0.05), (8192, 0.42), (12000, 0.53)]


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """Main entry point: search within budget_s and return the best sequence."""
    seed = int(seed)
    budget_s = float(budget_s)
    start0 = time.monotonic()
    total_budget = max(30.0, budget_s - 40.0)
    global_deadline = start0 + total_budget + 25.0

    external = _get_height_sequence()
    external_n = int(external.size) if external is not None else None

    print(f"budget={budget_s:.1f}s external_n={external_n}")

    # Initial fallback: central-binomial prefix of moderate length.
    fallback = _normalize_nonnegative(_prefix_sequence(1024), 1e-12)
    fallback_score = _score_fft(fallback)
    best_seq = fallback
    best_score = fallback_score

    candidates = []
    candidates.append((best_score, best_seq))

    stages = _stage_lengths(budget_s, external_n)
    for n, frac in stages:
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now >= global_deadline:
            break
        # Do not spend more than remaining; cap chunk by planned fraction.
        chunk = min(float(global_deadline - now), max(30.0, frac * total_budget))
        stage_deadline = now + chunk
        print(f"stage n={n} time={chunk:.1f}s")
        seq, sc = _search_length(int(n), stage_deadline, seed + int(n))
        if seq is not None and np.isfinite(sc):
            candidates.append((sc, seq))
            if sc < best_score:
                best_seq, best_score = seq, sc
                print(f"  new best (fft) = {best_score:.8f} len={best_seq.size}")

    # Final exact evaluation of the best candidate (and one fallback if needed).
    def _exact_score_list(seq_view):
        try:
            lst = [float(x) for x in seq_view]
            val = float(evaluate_sequence(lst))
            return lst, val
        except Exception:
            return None, np.inf

    if best_seq is None or best_seq.size == 0:
        best_seq = _normalize_nonnegative(_prefix_sequence(1024), 1e-12)

    final_list, final_score = _exact_score_list(best_seq)
    if final_list is not None and np.isfinite(final_score):
        best_seq = np.asarray(final_list, dtype=np.float64)
        best_score = final_score
        print(f"final exact score = {best_score:.8f}, length = {best_seq.size}")
    else:
        # If exact evaluation fails, try a smaller robust fallback.
        candidates.sort(key=lambda tuple_item: tuple_item[0])
        ok = False
        for _, seq_view in candidates:
            if seq_view is None:
                continue
            lst2, sc2 = _exact_score_list(seq_view)
            if lst2 is not None and np.isfinite(sc2):
                best_seq = np.asarray(lst2, dtype=np.float64)
                best_score = sc2
                ok = True
                break

        if not ok:
            final_list, final_score = _exact_score_list(fallback)
            if final_list is not None and np.isfinite(final_score):
                best_seq = np.asarray(final_list, dtype=np.float64)
                best_score = final_score
            else:
                return [1.0] * 64

    # Final sanitize: non-negative float list, no NaN/inf, bounded.
    out = []
    for x in best_seq:
        y = float(x)
        if not np.isfinite(y):
            y = 0.0
        if y < 0.0:
            y = 0.0
        if y > 1000.0:
            y = 1000.0
        out.append(y)

    if not out:
        out = [1.0] * 64

    return out
```
